   435	impl super::runner::Runner<'_> {
   436	    /// Authoritative adapters feed an ancestor-ordered batch to the ordinary
   437	    /// insert/update path; captures always resolve inside this transaction.
   438	    pub fn seed_server_rows(
   439	        &mut self,
   440	        rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>,
   441	    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   442	        if !self.context.is_system() || self.device {
   443	            return Err(RuntimeError::Input(
   444	                "server seed requires system authority".into(),
   445	            ));
   446	        }
   447	        let order = self.schema.through_order();
   448	        let mut rows: Vec<_> = rows.into_iter().collect();
   449	        rows.sort_by_key(|(n, _)| order.iter().position(|x| x == n));
   450	        let count = rows.len() as u64;
   451	        for (name, row) in rows {
   452	            super::timeline::check_import(self.schema, &name)?;
   453	            // Adoption rewrites are system imports of stored images (LLP 3000.015 §1).
   454	            self.upsert(
   455	                &name,
   456	                &row.field("id")?.clone(),
   457	                row.import_input(&super::ImportInput(())),
   458	            )?;
   459	        }
   460	        self.check_final_exists()?;
   461	        Ok(count)
   462	    }
   463	
   464	    /// A bounded child-first sweep on the common Rows interface.
   465	    pub fn sweep_server_rows(&mut self, limit: usize, expiry_after: &mut BTreeMap<String, Vec<Value>>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   466	        if !self.context.is_system() || self.device {
   467	            return Err(RuntimeError::Input(
   468	                "server sweep requires system authority".into(),
   469	            ));
   470	        }
   471	        let now = self
   472	            .context
   473	            .now
   474	            .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Input("sweep clock is not pinned".into()))?;
   475	        let mut count = 0;
   476	        let mut visited = 0;
   477	        let tables = resume_expiry_tables(self.schema.through_order().into_iter().rev().collect(), expiry_after);
   478	        for name in tables {
   479	            let Some((index, _)) = self.schema.tables[&name].expiry_index() else {
   480	                continue;
   481	            };
   482	            let due = Bounds { gte: Some(Value::Int(i64::MIN)), lte: Some(Value::Int(now)), ..Default::default() };
   483	            let mut bounds = due.clone();
   484	            bounds.after = expiry_after.get(&name).cloned();
   485	            let mut wrapped = bounds.after.is_none();
   486	            while visited < limit {
   487	                let batch = (limit - visited).min(256);
   488	                let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, scope| {
   489	                    runner
   490	                        .tx
   491	                        .scan(scope, &name, index, &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, batch)
   492	                })?;
   493	                if page.is_empty() {
   494	                    expiry_after.remove(&name);
   495	                    if !wrapped && visited < limit {
   496	                        bounds = due.clone();
   497	                        wrapped = true;
   498	                        continue;
   499	                    }
   500	                    break;
   501	                }
   502	                let page_len = page.len();
   503	                for (row, key) in page {
   504	                    bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   505	                    if let Some(after) = &bounds.after { expiry_after.insert(name.clone(), after.clone()); }
   506	                    visited += 1;
   507	                    mark_expiry_table(expiry_after, &name);
   508	                    match self.retire_expired_row(&name, &row) {
   509	                        Ok(true) => count += 1,
   510	                        Ok(false) => {},
   511	                        Err(RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_REFERENCED" | "E_PARENT_HAS_CHILDREN", .. }) => {},
   512	                        Err(error) => return Err(error),
   513	                    }
   514	                }
   515	                if page_len < batch {
   516	                    expiry_after.remove(&name);
   517	                    if !wrapped && visited < limit {
   518	                        bounds = due.clone();
   519	                        wrapped = true;
   520	                        continue;
   521	                    }
   522	                    break;
   523	                }
   524	            }
   525	        }
   526	        Ok(count)
   527	    }
   528	}

    13	
    14	#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
    15	pub(crate) enum Origin {
    16	    Source,
    17	    Maintained,
    18	}
    19	
    20	#[derive(Clone)]
    21	pub(super) struct FinalExistsWitness {
    22	    table: String,
    23	    row: Value,
    24	    constraint: usize,
    25	}
    26	
    27	#[path = "write_references.rs"]
    28	mod references;
    29	#[path = "write_target.rs"]
    30	pub(crate) mod target;
    31	
    32	impl Runner<'_> {
    33	    pub(super) fn insert(&mut self, table: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    34	        self.insert_mode(table, row, false).map(|_| ())
    35	    }
    36	
    37	    pub(super) fn insert_if_absent(&mut self, table: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    38	        self.insert_mode(table, row, true)
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    fn insert_mode(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: Supplied, if_absent: bool) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    42	        if self.device && self.schema.tables[table_name].not_before.is_some() {
    43	            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(
    44	                "a mutation touching a `not before` table runs on the server".into(),
    45	            ));
    46	        }
    47	        if self.device && if_absent && self.schema.tables[table_name].through.is_some() {
    48	            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("insert if absent on a read-through child queues without a row".into()));
    49	        }
    50	        self.target_attempt(table_name, |runner| {
    51	            let mark = runner.meter.mark();
    52	            let result = runner.insert_attempt(table_name, row, if_absent, None).map_err(|error| error.authorization_context(table_name, Effect::Insert));
    53	            // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — denied direct allocations hide deferred
    54	            // read witnesses and counter work just as NOT_FOUND hides targets.
    55	            if matches!(&result, Err(RuntimeError::Rule { effect: Effect::Insert, .. } | RuntimeError::AuthorizationBudget { .. }))
    56	                && runner.schema.tables.get(table_name).is_some_and(|table| !table.sequences.is_empty()) {
    57	                runner.meter.restore(mark);
    58	            }
    59	            result
    60	        })
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    fn insert_attempt(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: Supplied, if_absent: bool, upsert_index: Option<&str>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    64	        self.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Insert)?;
    65	        self.refuse_receipt_fields(table_name, &row, true)?;
    66	        self.refuse_recorded(table_name, &row)?;
    67	        let supplied_sequence = super::sequences::refuse_fields(&self.schema.tables[table_name], table_name, &row);
    68	        // A directly supplied field is a payload error before any lookup;
    69	        // only an upsert's copied address waits for insert authority.
    70	        if upsert_index.is_none() && supplied_sequence.is_err() { return supplied_sequence.map(|_| false); }
    71	        let supplied = row.clone();
    72	        let row = self.with_defaults(table_name, row)?;
    73	        if self.schema.tables[table_name].ephemeral.is_some() { self.meter.heap(row.heap_bytes())?; }
    74	        let row = row.ephemeral(&self.schema.tables[table_name], self.context.now.unwrap_or(0))?;
    75	        // @ref LLP 3000.014#4-the-capture — reserve, then stamp the parent capture before any rule sees the image.
    76	        self.reserve_capture(&self.schema.tables[table_name])?;
    77	        let row = self.capture_insert(table_name, row)?;
    78	        let table = &self.schema.tables[table_name];
    79	        let independent = !table.sequences.is_empty() && !super::rules::deciding_fields(table_name, table, &table.rules.insert)
    80	            .iter().any(|field| table.sequences.contains_key(field));
    81	        // An independent denial must never consult a hidden read witness.
    82	        let authorized = independent && self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)?;
    83	        if independent && !authorized {
    84	            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "insert rule") });
    85	        }
    86	        if !table.sequences.is_empty() {
    87	            // Admission reserves the worst case before either read verdict;
    88	            // restoring a refusal alone cannot hide a boundary abort.
    89	            // An independent insert rule was already prepaid and evaluated.
    90	            // Only still-pending checks belong in this preflight allowance.
    91	            let insert_checks = if authorized { 0 } else { 1 + u64::from(upsert_index.is_some() && supplied_sequence.is_ok()) };
    92	            self.reserve_sequence_authorization(table, &row, insert_checks)?;
    93	        }
    94	        // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — an unreadable allocation cannot expose counter,
    95	        // uniqueness or cap state, even to a principal allowed to insert.
    96	        if !self.sequence_insert_readable(table_name, &row)? {
    97	            self.refusing_clause(table_name, "sequence read authority");
    98	            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "sequence read authority") });
    99	        }
   100	        if let Err(error) = supplied_sequence {
   101	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — absent upserts decide insert authority before field diagnostics.
   102	            // A target key may supply an ordinal even when the patch does not.
   103	            if !authorized && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)? {
   104	                return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "insert rule") });
   105	            }
   106	            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { error });
   107	        }
   108	        if upsert_index.is_some() {
   109	            self.authorize_fields(table_name, &row, &supplied).map_err(|_| self.no_write_target())?;
   110	        } else { self.authorize_fields(table_name, &row, &supplied)?; }
   111	        // The image copy an event slot costs is the writer's own supplied bytes (LLP 3000.015 §1).
   112	        let image_bytes = row.heap_bytes();
   113	        let (row, sequence_full) = self.sequence_fields(table_name, row)?;
   114	        let row = self.receipt_insert_fields(table_name, row)?;
   115	        if let Some(index) = upsert_index {
   116	            if self.device { self.prove_upsert_insert(table_name, index, &row)?; }
   117	            // Preserve upsert's target preflight before the ordinary insert checks;
   118	            // only this first authority refusal is an undisclosed target miss.
   119	            if !authorized && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)? { return Err(self.no_write_target()); }
   120	            if sequence_full { return Err(super::sequences::exhausted()); }
   121	            // Engine event slots are stamped after predecessor/sequence checks,
   122	            // just as on an ordinary insert; preflight validates authored fields.
   123	            if let Err(error) = super::validate::validate_before_slots(self.schema, table_name, &row) {
   124	                return Err(if matches!(error, RuntimeError::Input(_)) && !self.context.is_system() { self.no_write_target() } else { error });
   125	            }
   126	        }
   127	        if !authorized && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)? {
   128	            return Err(self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "insert rule"));
   129	        }
   130	        // Retry keys independent of engine allocation still identify a no-op
   131	        // at the ceiling. Never use the clamped authority candidate as a key.
   132	        if sequence_full && if_absent {
   133	            let table = &self.schema.tables[table_name];
   134	            for (name, index) in table.indexes.iter().filter(|(_, index)| {
   135	                index.unique && index.columns().iter().all(|column| !table.sequences.contains_key(column))
   136	            }) {
   137	                let key = index_values(index, &row);
   138	                if key.contains(&Value::Null) { continue; }
   139	                if self.collision_lookup(table_name, name, index, &key, None, Effect::Insert)?.is_some() {
   140	                    self.observe_sequence_columns(table_name, &row, &index.columns(), true)?;
   141	                    return Ok(false);
   142	                }
   143	            }
   144	        }
   145	        if sequence_full { return Err(super::sequences::exhausted()); }
   146	        let row = self.record_insert(table_name, row)?;
   147	        let row = self.with_asset_facts(table_name, row, None, None)?;
   148	        if if_absent {
   149	            for (name, index) in table.indexes.iter().filter(|(_, index)| index.unique) {
   150	                let key = index_values(index, &row);
   151	                if key.iter().any(|value| *value == Value::Null) {
   152	                    continue;
   153	                }
   154	                if self.collision_lookup(table_name, name, index, &key, None, Effect::Insert)?.is_some() {
   155	                    self.observe_sequence_columns(table_name, &row, &index.columns(), true)?;
   156	                    return Ok(false);
   157	                }
   158	            }
   159	        }
   160	        let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
   161	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.5 — if-absent's insert arm needs the same image, unique-population and cap proof as upsert.
   162	        // Upsert mints its insert id; if-absent may instead supply one.
   163	        // Only a freshly minted id can skip the primary-key absence proof.
   164	        let minted = self.context.minted_ids.contains(&id);
   165	        let proof = if !self.device { None } else if minted {
   166	            table.indexes.iter().find(|(name, index)| {
   167	                    name.as_str() != "byId" && index.unique && !index_values(index, &row).contains(&Value::Null)
   168	                })
   169	                .map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).or_else(|| {
   170	                    table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Cap { .. })).then_some("byId")
   171	                })
   172	        } else { Some("byId") };
   173	        if if_absent && let Some(index) = proof.or(self.device.then_some("byId")) {
   174	            self.prove_upsert_insert(table_name, index, &row)?;
   175	        }
   176	        // An upsert has already resolved this exact address (or minted the id)
   177	        // and reserved both target proofs. Retiring an expired predecessor
   178	        // uses that same allowance, not a third read-rule reservation.
   179	        let collision_probes = if upsert_index.is_some() { 0 } else { super::rules::rule_probes(&table.rules.read) };
   180	        let has_through_children = self.schema.children(table_name).next().is_some();
   181	        let has_children = has_through_children || self.schema.is_referenced(table_name);
   182	        let predecessor = self.authorized_scan(&[(MeterKind::RuleProbes, collision_probes)], |runner, allowance| {
   183	            let existing = runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, &id)?;
   184	            if let Some(row) = &existing {
   185	                if !runner.expired(table, row) { runner.visible_collision(table_name, row, Effect::Insert, "unique id")?; }
   186	                // An expired predecessor's read rule cannot veto actionable
   187	                // reference retirement. A declared read-through child is
   188	                // different: a non-reader cannot probe whether hidden
   189	                // physical children retain the predecessor.
   190	                else if has_through_children && !runner.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Read, None, row)? {
   191	                    return Err(runner.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "unique id"));
   192	                }
   193	            }
   194	            Ok(existing)
   195	        })?;
   196	        // @ref LLP 3000.039#6.4 — a logical device miss cannot prove there is
   197	        // no hidden, expired physical predecessor whose retirement would run
   198	        // reference actions. Controller-minted identities are the existing
   199	        // fresh-address proof; every other referenced same-id insert queues.
   200	        if self.device && predecessor.is_none() && has_children && table.is_expiring() && !minted {
   201	            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(format!(
   202	                "same-id insert into referenced {table_name} needs the server's physical predecessor check; queued without predicted rows"
   203	            )));
   204	        }
   205	        if predecessor.as_ref().is_none_or(|old| self.expired(table, old)) && has_children {
   206	            let mark = self.meter.mark();
   207	            if self.physical_children(table_name, &Value::Id(id.clone()))? {
   208	                // The bounded probes have completed. Their occupancy is not
   209	                // disclosed by the collision's logical meter or heap peak.
   210	                self.meter.restore(mark);
   211	                return Err(if predecessor.is_some() {
   212	                    RuntimeError::Constraint(format!("{table_name} already has a row with id {id}"))
   213	                } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "parent identity has stored children") });
   214	            }
   215	        }
   216	        if let Some(existing) = &predecessor {
   217	            if !self.expired(table, &existing) {
   218	                return Err(RuntimeError::Constraint(format!("{table_name} already has a row with id {id}")));
   219	            }
   220	            // The expired predecessor is internal authorization work, not a
   221	            // disclosed examination (LLP 3000.015 §§1–2).
   222	            self.retire_expired_row(table_name, existing)?;
   223	        }
   224	        // Retiring an expired predecessor can cascade a source row whose
   225	        // contribution determines this image's maintained columns. Initialize
   226	        // them from the post-retirement private totals. Maintained columns are
   227	        // forbidden from constraints and authority, so the earlier collision
   228	        // and authorization preflights need only the authored image.
   229	        let row = self.birth_columns(table_name, row)?;
   230	        super::validate::validate_before_slots(self.schema, table_name, &row)?;
   231	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.5 — an authored plain insert predicts unproved; a later refusal is then the server's
   232	        // to report. The engine's own timeline writes are not the author's prediction.
   233	        if let Some(index) = proof.filter(|_| {
   234	            !if_absent && upsert_index.is_none() && !self.unproved_write && self.timeline_authority.is_none()
   235	        }) {
   236	            // An earlier unknown fact already makes this prediction unproved.
   237	            // Otherwise the proof's own unknown fact is not the prediction's.
   238	            self.unproved_write = self.missing_fact.is_some();
   239	            if !self.unproved_write {
   240	                let beyond = self.beyond_horizon;
   241	                let proved = self.device_work(|runner| runner.prove_upsert_insert(table_name, index, &row));
   242	                (self.beyond_horizon, self.missing_fact) = (beyond, None);
   243	                match proved { Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(_)) => self.unproved_write = true, other => other? }
   244	            }
   245	        }
   246	        // Retirement released every logical key and cap slot. Treat the new
   247	        // image as a fresh insert: an unchanged secondary key can now belong
   248	        // to another live row and must be probed again.
   249	        self.check_constraints(table_name, None, &row, Effect::Insert)?;
   250	        self.commit_sequences(table_name, &row)?;
   251	        self.commit_receipt_counter(table_name, &row)?;
   252	        let row = self.prepare_event_slots(table_name, None, row, image_bytes)?;
   253	        validate_row(self.schema, table_name, &row)?;
   254	        let entries = self.maintain_scratch(self.contribution_heap(table), |runner| {
   255	            runner.tx.put(table_name, table, &row, None)
   256	        })?;
   257	        self.charge_source_indexes(table, None, Some(&row), entries)?;
   258	        self.row_changed(table_name, None, Some(&row), Origin::Source)?;
   259	        Ok(true)
   260	    }
   261	
   262	    pub(super) fn update(&mut self, table_name: &str, key: &Value, patch: Supplied) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   263	        if self.device && self.schema.tables[table_name].not_before.is_some() {
   264	            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(
   265	                "a mutation touching a `not before` table runs on the server".into(),
   266	            ));
   267	        }
   268	        self.target_attempt(table_name, |runner| {
   269	            runner.update_attempt(table_name, key, patch)
   270	        })
   271	    }
   272	
   273	    fn update_attempt(&mut self, table_name: &str, key: &Value, patch: Supplied) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   274	        self.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Update)?;
   275	        self.refuse_receipt_fields(table_name, &patch, false)?;
   276	        self.refuse_recorded(table_name, &patch)?;
   277	        super::sequences::refuse_fields(&self.schema.tables[table_name], table_name, &patch)?;
   278	        if patch.as_object()?.contains_key("id") {
   279	            return Err(RuntimeError::Input(format!("{table_name} update patch cannot change id")));
   280	        }
   281	        let id = key.as_str()?;
   282	        self.reserve_target_resolution(table_name, self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?, &patch, false)?;
   283	        let Some(old) = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| {
   284	            runner.fetch_write_target(allowance, table_name, id)
   285	        })? else {
   286	            return Err(self.write_miss(table_name, "byId", &[key.clone()]));
   287	        };
   288	        if self.device && self.schema.tables[table_name].sync.is_none() { return Err(self.write_miss(table_name, "byId", &[key.clone()])); }
   289	        let readable = !self.inactive_write_target(table_name, &old)?
   290	            && self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Read, None, &old)?;
   291	        let patch = self.ignore_hidden_immutable_patch(table_name, patch, readable)?;
   292	        let (new, copy) = self.merge_patch(table_name, &old, &patch, readable)?;
   293	        self.replace(table_name, old, new, readable, &patch, copy)
   294	    }
   295	
   296	    pub(super) fn upsert(&mut self, table_name: &str, key: &Value, row: Supplied) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   297	        if self.device && self.schema.tables[table_name].not_before.is_some() {
   298	            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(
   299	                "a mutation touching a `not before` table runs on the server".into(),
   300	            ));
   301	        }
   302	        self.target_attempt(table_name, |runner| {
   303	            runner.upsert_attempt(table_name, key, row)
   304	        })
   305	    }
   306	
   307	    fn upsert_attempt(&mut self, table_name: &str, key: &Value, row: Supplied) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   308	        self.refuse_upsert_owned(table_name)?;
   309	        self.refuse_receipt_fields(table_name, &row, false)?;
   310	        self.refuse_recorded(table_name, &row)?;
   311	        super::sequences::refuse_fields(&self.schema.tables[table_name], table_name, &row)?;
   312	        let id = key.as_str()?;
   313	        if row.field("id")?.as_str()? != id {
   314	            return Err(RuntimeError::Input(format!("{table_name} upsert row id must equal its key")));
   315	        }
   316	        self.reserve_target_resolution(table_name, self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?, &row, true)?;
   317	        match self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| {
   318	            runner.fetch_write_target(allowance, table_name, id)
   319	        })? {
   320	            Some(old) => {
   321	                let readable = !self.inactive_write_target(table_name, &old)?
   322	                    && self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Read, None, &old)?;
   323	                let row = self.ignore_hidden_immutable_patch(table_name, row, readable)?;
   324	                // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — an existing row receives a patch; omitted columns keep their values.
   325	                let (new, copy) = self.merge_patch(table_name, &old, &row, readable)?;
   326	                self.replace(table_name, old, new, readable, &row, copy)
   327	            }
   328	            None if self.device && !self.upsert_absence_proven(table_name, "byId", &[key.clone()])? =>
   329	            {
   330	                Err(self.write_miss(table_name, "byId", &[key.clone()]))
   331	            }
   332	            None => self.insert_upsert(table_name, "byId", row),
   333	        }
   334	    }
   335	
   336	    pub(super) fn insert_upsert(&mut self, table_name: &str, addressed_index: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   337	        self.refuse_upsert_owned(table_name)?;
   338	        // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#5 — trusted fixture imports preserve the constraint layer.
   339	        if self.context.is_system() {
   340	            return self.insert_attempt(table_name, row, false, Some(addressed_index)).map(|_| ()).map_err(|error| match error {
   341	                RuntimeError::Input(message) => RuntimeError::Constraint(message),
   342	                other => other,
   343	            });
   344	        }
   345	        if self.schema.tables[table_name].through.is_some() {
   346	            // A read-through child's insert arm keeps the hidden-target word for every authority refusal.
   347	            return self.insert_attempt(table_name, row, false, Some(addressed_index)).map(|_| ()).map_err(|e| match e {
   348	                RuntimeError::Rule {..} | RuntimeError::Input(_) => self.no_write_target(), other => other,
   349	            });
   350	        }
   351	        match self.insert_attempt(table_name, row, false, Some(addressed_index)).map(|_| ()) {
   352	            Err(RuntimeError::Input(_)) => Err(self.no_write_target()),
   353	            result => result,
   354	        }
   355	    }
   356	
   357	    fn replace(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: Value, new: Value, readable: bool, patch: &Value, copy: u64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   358	        self.authorize_update_fields(table_name, &old, &new, patch, readable)?;
   359	        self.replace_authorized(table_name, old, new, readable, patch, copy)
   360	    }
   361	
   362	    /// `copy` is the merged image's lawful price, charged again for an engine slot copy.
   363	    pub(super) fn replace_authorized(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: Value, new: Value, readable: bool, patch: &Value, copy: u64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   364	        self.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Update)?;
   365	        self.refuse_receipt_fields(table_name, patch, false)?;
   366	        self.refuse_recorded(table_name, patch)?;
   367	        super::sequences::refuse_fields(&self.schema.tables[table_name], table_name, patch)?;
   368	        self.authorize_fields(table_name, &old, patch)?;
   369	        let definition = &self.schema.tables[table_name];
   370	        for path in definition.columns.keys().filter(|field| {
   371	            definition.engine_field(field) && field.as_str() != crate::schema::through::DEADLINE
   372	        }) {
   373	            if old.field(path).ok() != new.field(path).ok() {
   374	                return Err(if readable { RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_RECORDED_FIELD", message: format!("engine replacement changed stored attribution at {table_name}.{path} (guide maintains)") } } else { self.no_write_target() });
   375	            }
   376	        }
   377	        let mut new = new;
   378	        self.schema.tables[table_name].recompute_deadline(&mut new)?;
   379	        // @ref LLP 3000.017#2.2 — extending life re-admits the new image.
   380	        if self.schema.tables[table_name].stamp_ephemeral(&mut new, self.context.now.unwrap_or(0))?
   381	            && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &new)? {
   382	            return Err(if readable { RuntimeError::Rule { table: table_name.into(), effect: Effect::Update } } else { self.no_write_target() });
   383	        }
   384	        let new = self.with_asset_facts(table_name, new, Some(&old), (!readable).then_some(patch))?;
   385	        if let Err(error) = super::validate::validate_projection(self.schema, table_name, &new, self.device.then_some(self.context.viewer.as_str())) { return Err(if readable { error } else { self.no_write_target() }); }
   386	        self.check_constraints(table_name, Some(&old), &new, Effect::Update)?;
   387	        let new = self.prepare_event_slots(table_name, Some(&old), new, copy)?;
   388	        // A predicting device keeps another owner's omitted private field as an omission.
   389	        super::validate::validate_projection(self.schema, table_name, &new, self.device.then_some(self.context.viewer.as_str()))?;
   390	        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).expect("validated");
   391	        let entries = self.maintain_scratch(self.contribution_heap(table), |runner| {

   473	    /// Physically retire one row whose upper lifetime ended. Expiry bypasses
   474	    /// the root's authored delete rule and constraints, but it does not bypass
   475	    /// the schema's declared effects on rows that reference the root. A
   476	    /// read-through child still retains the expired parent physically.
   477	    // @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — expiry cleanup runs the same closure/restrict/set-null actions as an authored delete.
   478	    pub(super) fn retire_expired_row(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   479	        if self.physical_children(table_name, old.field("id")?)? { return Ok(false); }

   481	            self.apply_reference_actions(table_name, old)?;

   483	            // physical image so index and cap entries match what is stored.
   484	            let id = old.field("id")?.as_str()?;
   485	            self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, id))?

   488	            old.clone()

   493	
   494	    /// At a program commit's end, retire every source row the commit changed
   495	    /// or inserted whose lifetime has already ended: its final
   496	    /// image was never live at a commit boundary, so it leaves no row for a
   497	    /// later sweep to announce. Inside the commit the
   498	    /// row existed as written (an insert-then-update still works). Engine
   499	    /// bookkeeping, unmetered: the program's metering is already recorded.
   500	    /// Server execution and device prediction both run it so a predicted
   501	    /// final image cannot omit cascade, set-null or restrict effects. Its work,
   502	    /// returned, stays
   503	    /// linear in the rows retired: each aggregate row takes one net change
   504	    /// and each audience key is scanned for a live row once. Seeds and loads
   505	    /// ordinarily keep their rows for the sweep; row-keyed view sources use
   506	    /// `retire_seeded_view_sources` to preserve their count-basis contract.
   507	    // @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — a row whose lifetime ended by its own commit's end never exists at a commit boundary.
   508	    pub(crate) fn retire_expired_births(&mut self) -> Result<crate::meter::Metered, RuntimeError> {
   509	        let Some(now) = self.context.now else { return Ok(Default::default());
   510	        };
   511	        // @ref LLP 3000.036.000.000#3 — an expired final image never grants a new recipient the row's id.
   512	        // The tracker covers synced and unsynced source tables alike. Each
   513	        // distinct entry prepaid its tree node, strings and this vector slot;
   514	        // the rows-written ceiling bounds the commit-end walk.
   515	        // A row that existed before the commit and ends it already expired is
   516	        // retired too: accounted from the partitions it was in at the commit's
   517	        // start, it revokes there and announces nothing to a partition it only
   518	        // entered with its expiring image.
   519	        let mut changed: Vec<(String, String)> = std::mem::take(&mut self.expiry_rows).into_iter().collect();
   520	        if changed.is_empty() {
   521	            return Ok(Default::default());
   522	        }
   523	        // The log's order must not depend on a map's iteration order.
   524	        let order = self.schema.through_order();
   525	        changed.sort_by_key(|(name,id)| {
   526	            (std::cmp::Reverse(order.iter().position(|n| n == name)),id.clone(),
   527	            )
   528	        });
   529	        // Server execution already reserved this bookkeeping in the source
   530	        // writes. Device prediction instead runs the walk inside its separate
   531	        // bounded physical-work meter: exhausting that allowance queues the
   532	        // intent, never publishes a partial closure or refuses the write.
   533	        let meter = if self.device_work_active { None } else {
   534	            Some(std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter, crate::meter::Meter::new(crate::meter::Ceilings::unbounded(), crate::meter::Cut::Abort)))
   535	        };
   536	        self.retiring = Some(Default::default());
   537	        let retired = changed.iter().try_for_each(|(table_name, id)| {
   538	            let Some(row) = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, id))? else { return Ok(());
   539	            };
   540	            let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
   541	            if table.expired(&row, now) { self.retire_expired_row(table_name, &row).map(|_| ()) } else { Ok(()) }
   542	        });
   543	        let aggregates = self.retiring.take().map(|retiring| retiring.aggregates).unwrap_or_default();
   544	        let retired = retired.and_then(|()| self.apply_retired_aggregates(aggregates));
   545	        let work = meter.map(|meter| std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter, meter).used).unwrap_or_default();
   546	        retired.map(|()| work)
   547	    }
   548	
   549	    /// Seed writes historically retire only already-expired sources of
   550	    /// row-keyed views. Keep that narrow boundary while routing their removal
   551	    /// through the common action and net-maintenance path.
   552	    // @ref LLP 3000.039#6.5 — ordinary expired fixtures remain stored; derived row-view sources cannot revive after clock rollback.
   553	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   554	    pub(crate) fn retire_seeded_view_sources(&mut self) -> Result<crate::meter::Metered, RuntimeError> {
   555	        let schema = self.schema;
   556	        self.expiry_rows.retain(|(table, _)| schema.maintains.iter()
   557	            .any(|maintain| maintain.source == table.as_str() && maintain.row_key.is_some()));
   558	        self.retire_expired_births()
   559	    }

   561	    /// A column with a declared default takes it when the written row leaves
   562	    /// it out, before validation and before the asset facts, so the row the
   563	    /// rules and the constraints see is the complete one.
   564	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — omitted defaults use the clock and viewer of the host running the insert.
   565	    pub(super) fn with_defaults(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<Supplied, RuntimeError> {
   566	        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
   567	        if !table.defaults.keys().any(|name| {
   568	            row.as_object().is_ok_and(|fields| !fields.contains_key(name))
   569	        }) {
   570	            return Ok(row);
   571	        }
   572	        let row = row.defaults(table,self.context.now.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Input("write clock is not pinned".into()))?,&self.context.viewer)?;
   573	        self.meter.heap(row.heap_bytes())?;
   574	        Ok(row)
   575	    }

   578	    /// facts the server established about it (type, bytes, dimensions,
   579	    /// duration), never the client's word for them. The upload must be the
   580	    /// writer's own and of the column's kind and size. The system principal
   581	    /// may name an asset that was never uploaded: a fixture's placeholder,
   582	    /// metadata only, never served.
   583	    // Web stores hold received asset facts, never the authoritative upload ledger.
   584	    #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
   585	    fn with_asset_facts(&mut self, _table_name: &str, row: Value, _old: Option<&Value>, _hidden_patch: Option<&Value>) -> Result<Value, RuntimeError> {
   586	        Ok(row)
   587	    }

   589	    pub(super) fn refuse_upsert_owned(&self, table: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   590	        self.refuse_engine_owned(table, Effect::Insert)?;
   591	        self.refuse_engine_owned(table, Effect::Update)
   592	    }
   593	
   594	    /// Programs never write a maintained target; the engine does.
   595	    pub(super) fn refuse_engine_owned(&self, table: &str, effect: Effect) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   596	        // @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — pending member changes cannot rewrite settled requirements.
   597	        if self.device && effect != Effect::Insert && self.schema.receipts.iter().any(|f| f.members == table || f.runs == table) {
   598	            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("receipt membership changes and progress queue until settlement".into()));
   599	        }
   600	        self.refuse_receipt_table(table)?;
   601	        self.refuse_condition_write()?;
   602	        self.refuse_timeline_write(table, effect)?;
   603	        self.schema.refuse_private(table)?;
   604	        if self.schema.maintain_for(table).is_some() {
   605	            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{table} is a maintained aggregate; write its source rows instead")));
   606	        }
   607	        Ok(())
   608	    }
   609	
   610	    fn check_constraints(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: Option<&Value>, new: &Value, effect: Effect) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   611	        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).expect("validated");
   612	        let id = new.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
   613	        // @ref LLP 3000.039#4.1 — an action's own write consults no rule: probing the child's read rule would draw the shared allowance only where a child exists.
   614	        let readable = match old { Some(old) if effect == Effect::Update && !self.engine_action => {
   615	                !self.inactive_write_target(table_name, old)?
   616	                    && self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Read, None, old)?
   617	            }
   618	            _ => true };
   619	        if effect != Effect::Delete {
   620	            for (name, index) in table.indexes.iter().filter(|(_, index)| index.unique) {
   621	                // byId can only return this id (already checked on insert).
   622	                // An unchanged admitted unique key cannot acquire a collision.
   623	                if name == "byId" { continue; }
   624	                let key = index_values(index, new);
   625	                if old.is_some_and(|old| index_values(index, old) == key) { continue; }
   626	                if key.iter().any(|value| *value == Value::Null) {
   627	                    continue;
   628	                }
   629	                // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.5 — holding the target does not cover a different unique population.
   630	                if self.device && effect == Effect::Update && !self.device_work(|runner| {
   631	                        runner.upsert_absence_proven(table_name, name, &key)
   632	                    })? {
   633	                    return Err(self.write_miss(table_name, name, &key));
   634	                }
   635	                if let Some(existing) = self.collision_lookup(table_name, name, index, &key, Some(&id), effect)
   636	                    .map_err(|error| {
   637	                        if !readable && matches!(error, RuntimeError::Rule { .. }) { self.no_write_target() } else { error
   638	                        }
   639	                    })?
   640	                    && existing.field("id")?.as_str()? != id

codex

   517	        // start, it revokes there and announces nothing to a partition it only
   518	        // entered with its expiring image.
   519	        let mut changed: Vec<(String, String)> = std::mem::take(&mut self.expiry_rows).into_iter().collect();
   520	        if changed.is_empty() {
   521	            return Ok(Default::default());
   522	        }
   523	        // The log's order must not depend on a map's iteration order.
   524	        let order = self.schema.through_order();
   525	        changed.sort_by_key(|(name,id)| {
   526	            (std::cmp::Reverse(order.iter().position(|n| n == name)),id.clone(),
   527	            )
   528	        });
   529	        // Server execution already reserved this bookkeeping in the source
   530	        // writes. Device prediction instead runs the walk inside its separate
   531	        // bounded physical-work meter: exhausting that allowance queues the
   532	        // intent, never publishes a partial closure or refuses the write.
   533	        let meter = if self.device_work_active { None } else {
   534	            Some(std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter, crate::meter::Meter::new(crate::meter::Ceilings::unbounded(), crate::meter::Cut::Abort)))
   535	        };
   536	        self.retiring = Some(Default::default());
   537	        let retired = changed.iter().try_for_each(|(table_name, id)| {
   538	            let Some(row) = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, id))? else { return Ok(());
   539	            };
   540	            let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
   541	            if table.expired(&row, now) { self.retire_expired_row(table_name, &row).map(|_| ()) } else { Ok(()) }
   542	        });
   543	        let aggregates = self.retiring.take().map(|retiring| retiring.aggregates).unwrap_or_default();
   544	        let retired = retired.and_then(|()| self.apply_retired_aggregates(aggregates));
   545	        let work = meter.map(|meter| std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter, meter).used).unwrap_or_default();
   546	        retired.map(|()| work)
   547	    }
   548	
   549	    /// Seed writes historically retire only already-expired sources of
   550	    /// row-keyed views. Keep that narrow boundary while routing their removal
     1	//! Parent authority and lifetime checks shared by the storage adapters.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.014#4-the-capture — reserve before arm selection; resolve live, stamp, then authorize.
     3	use super::{rules, runner::Runner};
     4	use crate::{
     5	    error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError},
     6	    rows::{Bounds, Dir},
     7	    schema::{Effect, Table, through::*},
     8	    value::{Supplied, Value},
     9	};
    10	use std::collections::BTreeSet;
    11	
    12	/// Minted only here: the stamped image re-enters the supplied-input channel
    13	/// under reserve_capture's declared allowance.
    14	pub(crate) struct CaptureInput(());
    15	
    16	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
    17	pub(crate) enum ParentVerdict {
    18	    Readable,
    19	    Unreadable,
    20	    Absent,
    21	    Unknown,
    22	}
    23	
    24	impl Runner<'_> {
    25	    pub(super) fn reserve_capture(&mut self, table: &Table) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    26	        let Some(through) = &table.through else {
    27	            return Ok(());
    28	        };
    29	        if self.capture_prepaid {
    30	            return Ok(());
    31	        }
    32	        let parent = &self.schema.tables[&through.parent];
    33	        self.charge_price(crate::schema::reservations::capture_price(self.schema, table))?;
    34	        self.capture_prepaid = true;
    35	        self.read_tables.insert(through.parent.clone());
    36	        self.rule_dependencies(&parent.rules.read);
    37	        self.rule_dependencies(&table.rules.insert);
    38	        Ok(())
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    pub(super) fn capture_insert(&mut self, name: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<Supplied, RuntimeError> {
    42	        let result = self.capture_insert_inner(name, row);
    43	        self.trace_authorization(&result, name, Effect::Insert, "read through capture");
    44	        result
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    fn capture_insert_inner(
    48	        &mut self,
    49	        name: &str,
    50	        row: Supplied,
    51	    ) -> Result<Supplied, RuntimeError> {
    52	        let table = &self.schema.tables[name];
    53	        let Some(through) = &table.through else {
    54	            return Ok(row);
    55	        };
    56	        let mut row = row.into_value();
    57	        let id = row
    58	            .field(&through.reference)
    59	            .ok()
    60	            .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok())
    61	            .ok_or_else(|| self.rule_refusal(name, Effect::Insert, "read through parent reference"))?;
    62	        let parent = &self.schema.tables[&through.parent];
    63	        // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — the parent lookup is authorization work under the
    64	        // request's physical allowance; reserve_capture prepaid its logical charges.
    65	        let found = self.authorized_scan(&[], |runner, scope| runner.tx.get(scope, &through.parent, id))?.filter(|r| !self.expired(parent, r));
    66	        if self.device {
    67	            let covered = if let Some(found) = &found {
    68	                self.is_provisional(&through.parent, found)? || self.device_work(|runner| {
    69	                    let viewer = runner.context.viewer.clone();
    70	                    let now = runner.context.now.unwrap_or(0);
    71	                    runner.authorized_scan(&[], |runner, scope| runner.tx.recording_covered(&through.parent, found, &viewer, now, scope, &mut runner.meter))
    72	                })?
    73	            } else { false };
    74	            if !covered { return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(format!("read through .{} needs a live, covered {} parent; queued without a row", through.reference, through.parent))); }
    75	        }
    76	        let found = found.ok_or_else(|| self.rule_refusal(name, Effect::Insert, "read through parent missing or expired"))?;
    77	        stamp(parent, through, &found, &mut row)?;
    78	        table.recompute_deadline(&mut row)?;
    79	        if !self.context.is_system() {
    80	            let viewer = self.context.viewer.clone();
    81	            let readable = self.authorized_scratch(self.rule_scratch(&table.rules.read), &[], |runner, allowance| {
    82	                rules::evaluate(&table.rules.read, &viewer, None, &row, false, &mut super::read::PrefixProbe { runner, allowance })
    83	            })?;
    84	            if !readable {
    85	                // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#5 — only the developer trace names the unread parent.
    86	                self.refusing_phase(&through.parent, &format!("insert into {name} needs unread parent {} through .{}", through.parent, through.reference), "read rule");
    87	                return Err(RuntimeError::Rule { table: name.into(), effect: Effect::Insert });
    88	            }
    89	        }
    90	        Ok(row.capture_input(&CaptureInput(())))
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    pub(super) fn physical_children(
    94	        &mut self,
    95	        parent: &str,
    96	        id: &Value,
    97	    ) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    98	        self.physical_children_outside(parent, id, &BTreeSet::new())
    99	    }
   100	
   101	    /// Whether a read-through child remains outside a delete's cascade
   102	    /// closure. Children in `deleting` are removed first by deletion_order and
   103	    /// therefore do not physically retain their parent.
   104	    pub(super) fn physical_children_outside(
   105	        &mut self,
   106	        parent: &str,
   107	        id: &Value,
   108	        deleting: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>,
   109	    ) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   110	        let children: Vec<_> = self
   111	            .schema
   112	            .children(parent)
   113	            .map(|(n, _, _)| {
   114	                // Only identities from this child table can appear in its
   115	                // index scan. Identities from other closure tables cannot
   116	                // consume a row in this table's prefix.
   117	                let inside = deleting.iter().filter(|(table, _)| table == n).count();
   118	                (n.clone(), inside.saturating_add(1))
   119	            })
   120	            .collect();
   121	        // Reserve the complete schema-fixed probe set before its first seek.
   122	        // Never short-circuit on an occupied table, including at low ceilings.
   123	        // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — the seeks draw from the request's physical allowance; occupancy never selects the logical charge.
   124	        let heap = children.iter().map(|(name, limit)| super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[name]).saturating_mul(*limit as u64)).fold(0_u64, u64::saturating_add);
   125	        let probes = children.iter().map(|(_, limit)| *limit as u64).fold(0_u64, u64::saturating_add);
   126	        self.authorized_scan(&[(MeterKind::Examined, probes), (MeterKind::Steps, probes), (MeterKind::HeapBytes, heap)], |runner, scope| {
   127	            let mut occupied = false;
   128	            for (name, limit) in &children {
   129	                let rows = runner.tx.scan(scope, name, CHILDREN, &[id.clone()], &Bounds::default(), Dir::Asc, *limit)?;
   130	                occupied |= rows.into_iter().any(|(row, _)| row.field("id").ok().and_then(|id| id.as_str().ok())
   131	                    .is_none_or(|child| !deleting.contains(&(name.clone(), child.to_owned()))));
   132	            }
   133	            Ok(occupied)
   134	        })
   135	    }
   136	
   137	    // A deadline suffix skips an arbitrary expired backlog in one seek.
   138	    pub(super) fn refuse_live_children(
   139	        &mut self,
   140	        parent: &str,
   141	        id: &Value,
   142	    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   143	        let children: Vec<_> = self
   144	            .schema
   145	            .children(parent)
   146	            .map(|(n, _, _)| n.clone())
   147	            .collect();
   148	        if children.is_empty() { return Ok(()); }
   149	        let now = self
   150	            .context
   151	            .now
   152	            .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Input("delete clock is not pinned".into()))?;
   153	        let heap = children
   154	            .iter()
   155	            .map(|n| super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[n]).saturating_mul(3))
   156	            .fold(0_u64, u64::saturating_add);
   157	        // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — three schema-fixed seeks per child table, each drawn from the request's physical allowance.
   158	        self.authorized_scan(&[(MeterKind::Examined, children.len() as u64 * 3), (MeterKind::Steps, children.len() as u64 * 3), (MeterKind::HeapBytes, heap)], |runner, scope| {
   159	            if runner.device {
   160	                let occupied = runner.device_work(|runner| runner.current_children(parent, id, &children))?;
   161	                return if occupied { Err(runner.parent_has_children(parent)) } else { Ok(()) };
   162	            }
   163	            for name in &children {
   164	                let never = runner.tx.scan(scope, name, CHILDREN, &[id.clone(), Value::Null], &Bounds::default(), Dir::Asc, 1)?;
   165	                let finite = runner.tx.scan(scope, name, CHILDREN, &[id.clone()], &Bounds { gt: Some(Value::Int(now)), ..Default::default() }, Dir::Asc, 1)?;
   166	                if !never.is_empty() || !finite.is_empty() {
   167	                    return Err(runner.parent_has_children(parent));
   168	                }
   169	                // §3.3 forbids physical removal even when §6's live probe is empty.
   170	                // The author's implementation ruling keeps this case fail-closed.
   171	                if !runner.tx.scan(scope, name, CHILDREN, &[id.clone()], &Bounds::default(), Dir::Asc, 1)?.is_empty() {
   172	                    return Err(runner.parent_has_children(parent));
   173	                }
   174	            }
   175	            Ok(())
   176	        })
   177	    }
   178	
   179	    pub(super) fn parent_has_children(&self, parent: &str) -> RuntimeError {
   180	        RuntimeError::Structural {
   181	            code: "E_PARENT_HAS_CHILDREN",
   182	            message: format!("{parent} at {} has stored children; delete its live read-through children and sweep expired children, then retry `delete {parent}[id]` (guide rules)", self.statement_site),
   183	        }
   184	    }
   185	
   186	    // @ref LLP 3000.014#5 — retained history cannot terminally refuse a
   187	    // current delete. Mismatched, expired or uncovered witnesses must queue.
   188	    fn current_children(&mut self, parent: &str, id: &Value, children: &[String]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   189	        if children.is_empty() { return Ok(false); }
   190	        let uncertain = || RuntimeError::Prediction("parent deletion needs complete matching current child coverage; queued without a row".into());
   191	        self.meter.charge(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   192	        self.meter.heap(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[parent]))?;
   193	        // Device-only work over held state: the token counts it on the device's separate physical allowance.
   194	        let parent_id = id.as_str()?.to_owned();
   195	        let parent_row = self.authorized_scan(&[], |runner, scope| runner.tx.get(scope, parent, &parent_id))?.ok_or_else(uncertain)?;
   196	        for name in children {
   197	            let table = &self.schema.tables[name];
   198	            let through = table.through.as_ref().unwrap();
   199	            if !self.child_coverage(name, through, &parent_row)? { return Err(uncertain()); }
   200	            let table_scratch = self.meter.mark();
   201	            // Expected captures plus a reusable row/cursor, reserved before seeking.
   202	            self.meter.heap(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(table).saturating_mul(3))?;
   203	            let mut expected = crate::value::object([] as [(&str, Value); 0]);
   204	            stamp(&self.schema.tables[parent], through, &parent_row, &mut expected)?;
   205	            let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
   206	            loop {
   207	                let scratch = self.meter.mark();
   208	                self.meter.charge(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   209	                self.meter.step(through.fields.len() as u64 + 1)?;
   210	                let page = self.authorized_scan(&[], |runner, scope| runner.tx.scan_metered(scope, name, CHILDREN, &[id.clone()], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 1, &mut runner.meter))?;
   211	                let Some((row, key)) = page.into_iter().next() else { break };
   212	                if self.engine_action && self.tx.retained_row(name, &row)? { return Err(uncertain()); }
   213	                if !self.tx.retained_row(name, &row)? {
   214	                    if self.expired(table, &row)
   215	                        || !(self.tx.current_row(name, &row)? || self.is_provisional(name, &row)?)
   216	                        || !capture_matches(through, &expected, &row) { return Err(uncertain()); }
   217	                    return Ok(true);
   218	                }
   219	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key).map(|key| key.into_iter().skip(1).collect());
   220	                self.meter.release_heap(scratch);
   221	            }
   222	            self.meter.release_heap(table_scratch);
   223	        }
   224	        Ok(false)
   225	    }
   226	}
   227	
   228	pub(super) fn capture_matches(through: &Through, expected: &Value, row: &Value) -> bool {
   229	    through.fields.keys().map(String::as_str).chain([PARENT_DEADLINE])
   230	        .all(|field| row.field(field).ok() == expected.field(field).ok())
   231	}
   232	
   233	pub(super) fn stamp(
   234	    parent: &Table,
   235	    through: &Through,
   236	    image: &Value,
   237	    row: &mut Value,
   238	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   239	    let image = crate::schema::row_image(image, parent);
   240	    if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = row {
   241	        for (capture, source) in &through.fields {
   242	            fields.insert(capture.clone(), image.field(source)?.clone());
   243	        }
   244	        fields.insert(
   245	            PARENT_DEADLINE.into(),
   246	            parent
   247	                .deadline(&image)
   248	                .map(Value::Int)
   249	                .unwrap_or(Value::Null),
   250	        );
   251	    }
   252	    Ok(())
   253	}

   157	/// Text forbids C0 controls except tab, LF and CR.
   158	pub fn disallowed_text_control(text: &str) -> Option<u32> {
   159	    text.chars().find(|c| c.is_control() && !matches!(c, '\t' | '\n' | '\r')).map(|c| c as u32)
   160	}
   161	
   162	pub(crate) fn fits(kind: &ColumnType, value: Option<&Value>) -> bool {
   163	    match (kind, value) {
   164	        (ColumnType::Optional(_), None | Some(Value::Null)) => true,
   165	        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), Some(value)) => fits(inner, Some(value)),
   166	        (ColumnType::List { of, max } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, max, .. }, Some(Value::Array(values))) => values.len() <= *max as usize && values.iter().all(|value| fits(of, Some(value))),
   167	        // @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — typed references do not imply target existence
   168	        (ColumnType::Id | ColumnType::Principal | ColumnType::Ref { .. }, Some(Value::Id(_))) => true,
   169	        (ColumnType::Text { max }, Some(Value::String(text))) => text.chars().count() <= *max as usize,
   170	        (ColumnType::Bytes { max }, Some(Value::Bytes(bytes))) => bytes.len() <= *max as usize,
   171	        (ColumnType::Image { .. } | ColumnType::Video { .. } | ColumnType::File { .. }, Some(Value::Map(fields))) => fields.get("id").is_some_and(|id| matches!(id, Value::Id(_))),
   172	        (ColumnType::Enum { variants }, Some(Value::String(value))) => variants.contains(value),
   173	        (ColumnType::Int | ColumnType::Time | ColumnType::Decimal { .. } | ColumnType::Money { .. }, Some(Value::Int(value))) => (I53_MIN..=I53_MAX).contains(value),
   174	        // @ref LLP 3000.040#14-where-the-bound-is-enforced — the slot's bound is part of its type: every stored write and argument meets it here.
   175	        (ColumnType::IntRange { min, max }, Some(Value::Int(value))) => (I53_MIN..=I53_MAX).contains(value) && (*min..=*max).contains(value),
   176	        (ColumnType::Bool, Some(Value::Bool(_))) => true,
   177	        (ColumnType::Json { max_bytes }, Some(value)) => canonical_json(value).len() <= *max_bytes as usize,
   178	        _ => false,
   179	    }
   180	}

   182	/// Validate the arguments and fill each omitted optional with `null`, so
   183	/// a caller that leaves `c` out reads page one.
   184	#[cfg(not(all(target_arch = "wasm32", feature = "web", not(feature = "sqlite"))))]
   185	pub fn validate_inputs(schema: &Schema, program: &Program, inputs: &mut BTreeMap<String, Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   186	    super::admission::check(schema, program)?;
   187	    validate_arguments(schema, program, inputs)
   188	}
   189	
   190	// @ref LLP 3000.002#proposed-device-representation-and-codec-redesign — the web predicts a server-admitted backend; the server and native hosts still check every call.
   191	pub(super) fn validate_device_inputs(schema: &Schema, program: &Program, inputs: &mut BTreeMap<String, Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   192	    #[cfg(not(any(all(target_arch = "wasm32", feature = "web", not(feature = "sqlite")), feature = "web-device-check")))]
   193	    super::admission::check(schema, program)?;
   194	    validate_arguments(schema, program, inputs)
   195	}
   196	
   197	fn validate_arguments(schema: &Schema, program: &Program, inputs: &mut BTreeMap<String, Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   198	    for (name, kind) in &program.args {
   199	        if !inputs.contains_key(name) && (kind.is_optional() || matches!(kind, ArgType::Cursor)) {
   200	            inputs.insert(name.clone(), Value::Null);
   201	        }
   202	        let value = inputs.get(name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Input(format!("missing argument {name}")))?;
   203	        validate_arg(schema, kind, value, name)?;
   204	    }
   205	    if let Some(extra) = inputs.keys().find(|name| !program.args.contains_key(*name)) {
   206	        return Err(RuntimeError::Input(format!("{} takes no argument {extra}", program.name)));
   207	    }
   208	    Ok(())
   209	}
   210	
   211	pub fn validate_arg(schema: &Schema, kind: &ArgType, value: &Value, label: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   212	    if !kind.bounds_valid() {
   213	        return Err(RuntimeError::Input(format!("{label} has a minimum greater than its maximum")));
   214	    }
   215	    crate::value::validate_identities(value)?;
   216	    if contains_system_principal(value) {
   217	        return Err(RuntimeError::Input(format!("{label} cannot carry the reserved system principal")));
   218	    }
   219	    match kind {
   220	        ArgType::Cursor => match value {
   221	            Value::Null => Ok(()),
   222	            Value::String(token) => crate::cursor::decode_cursor(schema, token).map(|_| ()).map_err(|error| RuntimeError::Input(format!("{label}: {error}"))),
   223	            _ => Err(RuntimeError::Input(format!("{label} must be an opaque cursor string or null"))),
   224	        },
   225	        ArgType::Optional(_) if *value == Value::Null => Ok(()),
   226	        ArgType::Optional(inner) => validate_arg(schema, inner, value, label),
   227	        ArgType::Scalar => match value {
   228	            Value::Row(_) | Value::Map(_) => Err(RuntimeError::Input(format!("{label} must be a scalar"))),
   229	            _ => Ok(()),
   230	        },
   231	        ArgType::Text { max, min, format } => match value {
   232	            Value::String(text) => {
   233	                if let Some(code_point) = disallowed_text_control(text) {
   234	                    return Err(RuntimeError::Input(format!("{label} contains disallowed control character U+{code_point:04X}")));
   235	                }
   236	                if text.chars().count() > *max as usize {
   237	                    return Err(RuntimeError::Input(format!("{label} is longer than {max} characters")));
   238	                }
   239	                validate_text(label, text, *min, *format)
   240	            }
   241	            other => Err(RuntimeError::Input(format!("{label} must be text, not {}", describe(other)))),
   242	        },
   243	        ArgType::List { of, max } | ArgType::ListRange { of, max, .. } => match value {
   244	            Value::Array(items) => {
   245	                if let ArgType::ListRange { min, .. } = kind { validate_list_minimum(label, items.len(), *min)?; }

+            rows_written: price[3], heap_bytes: price[4], output_bytes: price[5],

+        let mut runner = Runner::new(&rows, &schema, Context::system().at(1), Meter::new(ceilings, Cut::Abort), 0);

+    }

     r.table.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.reference == r.column)
